# Behavioral Contract Index

> Master index of all 1,967 behavioral contracts across 10 subsystems.
> Source of truth for BC count, status, and subsystem assignment.

> **D-443(b)(i) + D-444(e)(ii) documentary-historical exemption (per D-414(c)):** Pre-v2.06-cohort changelog entries (BC v1.78..v2.05) are documentary-historical-exempt per D-414(c); the sample-vs-exhaustive flag introduced at pass-62 fix burst (D-442(c)) applies forward-only from BC v2.06+. | [BC-7.07.001](ss-07/BC-7.07.001.md) | precompact-flush native WASM plugin fires synchronously on PreCompact; hermetic (STATE.md + git only); renews factory lock natively when held via the identity-gated shared function crates/factory-lock::renew_lock_if_holder() (no-op when absent, when holder mismatches the plugin's own resolved git identity, or when the recorded lock is already expired; emits factory.lock.renewal_indeterminate + log_warn when identity resolution itself fails on a self-held, non-expired lock — ADR-046 Decision 3/4); commits (local) with `PreCompact flush <cycle>/<step>` message; appends commit SHA to precompact-flush-log; pushes to remote; on append failure: SHA-pinned reset (`git reset --soft SHA_B^` when HEAD==SHA_B; no-reset + human-intervention exit 2 when HEAD!=SHA_B); exit 2 on commit failure (local; no network) or push failure (network); fail-open on crash | **active** | CAP-032 | S-18.04a, S-17.05 | v1.0 \| v1.1 \| v1.2 \| v1.3 \| v1.4 \| v1.5 \| v1.6 \| v1.7 \| v1.8 \| v1.9 \| v1.10 \| v1.11 \| v1.12 \| v1.13 \| v1.14 \| v1.15 \| v1.16 \| v1.17 \| v1.18 \| v1.19 (2026-08-25: ADR-046 v1.1 Companion Amendment 3 — identity-gated `renew_lock_if_holder` replaces identity-blind `renew_lock`;
